```
先在反编译源码里 grep 全面定位 luck 消费点(已知线索:Terraria.GameContent/Luck.cs 的 RollLuck 系列;NPC 掉落 DropRules;Main.DamageVar;Player.cs 中 luck 使用;Projectile.cs 钓鱼 FishingBobber/FishingCheck.cs;旅商 SetupTravelShop Main.cs:1251 附近;金动物生成)。grep 建议:

- grep -n "luck" Terarria1456/Terraria/Projectile.cs | head -40
- grep -n "luck" Terarria1456/Terraria/Player.cs | grep -in "fish\|damage\|vanity\|shop\|merchant" 
- grep -rn "luck" Terarria1456/Terraria/GameContent/ItemDropRules/ | head
- 金动物:grep -n "golden\|Golden" Terarria1456/Terraria/NPC.cs | grep -i "critter\|300\|luck" 以及 spawner 文件(可能叫 Terraria/GameContent/NPCSpawning 或 NPC.cs 的 spawn 调度),找金瓢虫/金小动物生成概率是否受 luck 影响。

必核清单(每项:原版设定+源码行号 / 本仓库 file:line / 状态 ✅对齐|⚠️数值偏差|❌未实现|🔀逻辑不同|❓存疑):
1. 掉落率:DropRules 各规则族(CommonDrop/OneFromOptions 等)如何进 Luck.RollLuck;NotScalingWithLuck 族的直掷。
2. 玩家受伤浮动:Main.DamageVar / Player 受伤处 luck 的影响(伤害 ± 浮动公式,注意原版是 DamageVar(damage, -luck))。
3. 钓鱼:luck 是否影响 FishingCheck(钓上鱼的品质/稀有度/垃圾率),公式与行号。
4. 旅商(Traveling Merchant)稀有货品:SetupTravelShop 的 RollLuck 稀有度档位与数值。
5. 金色小动物(golden critter)生成概率是否受 luck 影响(源码在哪,数值)。
6. 其他你 grep 到的 luck 消费点(如 NPC 掉钱金额 NPCLoot_DropMoney、材料掉落数量、玩家对 NPC 伤害等),逐条列出。
```

```
	public static int DamageVar(float dmg, float luck = 0f)
	{
		if (DebugOptions.NoDamageVar)
		{
			return (int)dmg;
		}
		float num = dmg * (1f + (float)rand.Next(-15, 16) * 0.01f);
		if (luck > 0f)
		{
			if (rand.NextFloat() < luck)

		else if (luck < 0f && rand.NextFloat() < 0f - luck)
		{
			float num3 = dmg * (1f + (float)rand.Next(-15, 16) * 0.01f);
			if (num3 < num)
			{
				num = num3;
			}
		}
		return (int)Math.Round(num);
	}

		}
```

```
    48	export function rollLuck(luck: number, range: number, rand: () => number = Math.random): number {
    49	  if (luck > 0 && rand() < luck) return nextInt(Math.floor(range / 2) + nextInt(range - Math.floor(range / 2), rand), rand);
    50	  if (luck < 0 && rand() < -luck) return nextInt(range + nextInt(range, rand), rand);
    51	  return nextInt(range, rand);
    52	}
    53

    55	export function rollBadLuck(luck: number, range: number, rand: () => number = Math.random): number {

   216	const TORCH_TILE_ID = (() => {
   217	  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) if (TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet === 4) return i;
   218	  return -1;
   219	})();
   220	const GNOME_TILE_ID = (() => {
   221	  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) if (TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet === 567) return i;
   222	  return -1;
   223	})();
   224	
   225	/** 地精在否（SceneMetrics.cs:597-600：_tileCounts[567]>0，169×124 窗口任一即真） */
   226	function scanGnome(st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number): boolean {
   227	  if (GNOME_TILE_ID < 0) return false;
   228	  const x0 = Math.max(0, tx - GNOME_WINDOW_X), x1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, tx + GNOME_WINDOW_X);
   229	  const y0 = Math.max(0, ty - GNOME_WINDOW_Y), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, ty + GNOME_WINDOW_Y);
   230	  for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
   231	    for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
   232	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
   233	      if (st.flags[i] && st.type[i] === GNOME_TILE_ID) return true;
   234	    }
   235	  }
   236	  return false;
   237	}
   238	
   239	/** 扫描火炬窗口 + 地精窗口，算出 torchLuck 与 gnome 在否（1:1 净效果版） */
   240	export function scanLuckScene(input: LuckScanInput): { torchLuck: number; hasGardenGnome: boolean } {
   241	  const { store: st, tx, ty } = input;
   242	  if (!input.belowSurface) {
   243	    // TryRecalculatingTorchLuck :17592-17598：地表直接 ConsumeCounters → torchLuck=0
   244	    return { torchLuck: 0, hasGardenGnome: scanGnome(st, tx, ty) };
   245	  }
   246	
   247	  // 火炬窗口计数（:17604-17640；nearbyTorches 燃烧计数只喂 Torch God 触发，
   248	  // 由 TorchGod.ts 自扫，此处不重复累计）
   249	  const nearby = new Array<boolean>(TORCH_STYLE_COUNT).fill(false);
   250	  let dryCoral = false;
   251	  if (TORCH_TILE_ID >= 0) {
   252	    const s0x = Math.max(10, tx - TORCH_WINDOW), s1x = Math.min(st.w - 10, tx + TORCH_WINDOW);
   253	    const s0y = Math.max(10, ty - TORCH_WINDOW), s1y = Math.min(st.h - 10, ty + TORCH_WINDOW);
   254	    for (let y = s0y; y <= s1y; y++) {
   255	      for (let x = s0x; x <= s1x; x++) {
   256	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
   257	        if (!st.flags[i] || st.type[i] !== TORCH_TILE_ID || st.frameX[i] < 0 || st.frameY[i] < 0) continue;
   258	        const style = Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 22);
   259	        if (style < TORCH_STYLE_COUNT) {
   260	          nearby[style] = true;
   261	          // :17629-17634：珊瑚炬非水中 → dryCoral（下文按"错群系"计负）
   262	          if (style === TORCH_STYLE.Coral && (st.liquid[i] === 0 || st.liquidType[i] !== 1)) dryCoral = true;
   263	        }
   264	      }
   265	    }
   266	  }
   267	  if (input.heldTorchStyle !== null && input.heldTorchStyle < TORCH_STYLE_COUNT) {
   268	    nearby[input.heldTorchStyle] = true;   // UpdateTorchLuck_ConsumeCountersAndCalculate :17775-17778
   269	  }
   270	
   271	  // ---- UpdateTorchLuck_ConsumeCountersAndCalculate（:17771-17939）----
   272	  // num2=good（正确群系火炬加分项）num=bad（错群系火炬负分项）
   273	  const z = input.zones;
   274	  let good = 0;
   275	  let bad = 0;
   276	  if (!z.dungeon && !z.temple) {
   277	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Ice]) { good += z.snow ? 1 : 0; bad += z.snow ? 0 : 1; }
   278	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Desert]) { good += z.desert ? 1 : 0; bad += z.desert ? 0 : 1; }
   279	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Coral]) {
   280	      if (z.ocean) good += 1;          // :17813-17815 海洋深度带内 = 正确群系
   281	      else if (dryCoral) bad += 1;     // :17816-17818 干珊瑚炬 = 错群系
   282	    }
   283	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Jungle]) { good += z.jungle ? 1 : 0; bad += z.jungle ? 0 : 1; }
   284	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Mushroom]) { good += z.glowshroom ? 1 : 0; bad += z.glowshroom ? 0 : 1; }
   285	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Corrupt]) { good += z.corrupt ? 1 : 0; bad += z.corrupt ? 0 : 1; }
   286	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Cursed] && z.corrupt) good += 1;   // :17828-17830
   287	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Crimson]) { good += z.crimson ? 1 : 0; bad += z.crimson ? 0 : 1; }
   288	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Ichor] && z.crimson) good += 1;    // :17831-17833
   289	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Hallowed]) { good += z.hallow ? 1 : 0; bad += z.hallow ? 0 : 1; }
   290	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Bone] && !z.snow && !z.desert && !z.corrupt && !z.crimson && !z.hallow && !z.jungle) {
   291	      good += 0.5;                                            // :17834-17836
   292	    }
   293	    if (nearby[TORCH_STYLE.Torch]) {
   294	      if (z.snow) bad += 2;                                    // :17837-17841
   295	      else if (z.desert || z.corrupt || z.crimson || z.hallow) bad += 0.5;
   296	    }
   297	  }
   298	  let torchLuck = 0;
   299	  if (good >= 1) torchLuck += 1;
   300	  else if (good > 0) torchLuck += 0.5;
   301	  if (bad >= 2) torchLuck += -1.5;
   302	  else if (bad >= 1) torchLuck += -1;
   303	  else if (bad > 0) torchLuck += -0.5;
   304	  if (torchLuck < 0) torchLuck = 0;   // :17919-17921
   305	  return { torchLuck, hasGardenGnome: scanGnome(st, tx, ty) };
   306	}
```

```
    case 'DontStarveIsUp': return false;

      const me = ctx.moonEvent;

// ============ 规则求值（ItemDropResolver.cs 语义） ============
/** Player.RollLuck(denom, luck=0) ≡ rng.Next(denom) */
function rollLuck(ctx: NpcDropCtx, denom: number, num: number): boolean {
  if (denom <= 1) return true;
  const luck = ctx.luck ?? 0;
  if (luck !== 0 && ctx.rng.next() < Math.abs(luck)) {
    // Luck.RollLuck（Luck.cs:6）：幸运 → 先掷 [denom/2, denom) 再掷（<num 更易命中）；
    // 负幸运反向 → [denom, denom*2) 更难命中
    const inner = luck > 0
      ? ctx.rng.int(Math.floor(denom / 2), denom - 1)
      : ctx.rng.int(denom, denom * 2 - 1);
    return ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1, inner) - 1) < num;
  }
  return ctx.rng.int(0, denom - 1) < num;
}

/** 规则掷骰统一入口：JSON 的 noLuck 标记（NotScalingWithLuck 族）此前被忽略——
 *  CommonDropNotScalingWithLuck.cs:17 / OneFromOptionsNotScaledWithLuckDropRule.cs:115
 *  用 info.rng.Next(denom)<num（不进 Luck.RollLuck），必须走直掷而非 rollLuck，
 *  否则幸运玩家在这些规则上白吃加成（如 EoC 专家 Ore 池 NormalvsExpert…NotScalingWithLuck）。
 *  denom<=1 直接过与 rollLuck 同约定（结果恒真，仅差一次空抽） */
function tryRoll(ctx: NpcDropCtx, rule: Rule): boolean {
  const denom = rule.denom ?? 1;
  const num = rule.num ?? 1;
  if (rule.noLuck) {
    if (denom <= 1) return true;
    return ctx.rng.int(0, denom - 1) < num;
  }
  return rollLuck(ctx, denom, num);
}

function pushDrop(out: NpcDropOutcome, id: number, n: number) {
  if (n > 0) out.drops.push({ id, n });
}

function condPass(rule: Rule, ctx: NpcDropCtx): boolean | null {
  if (!rule.cond) return true;
  const pass = evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx);
  return pass;
}

function evalRule(rule: Rule, ctx: NpcDropCtx, out: NpcDropOutcome): State {
  let state: State;
  switch (rule.k) {
    case 'common': {
      if (tryRoll(ctx, rule)) {
        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'rerolls': {
      // CommonDropWithRerolls：rerolls+1 次掷骰任一成功（继承 RollLuck，无 noLuck 变体）
      let hit = false;
      for (let i = 0; i <= (rule.rerolls ?? 0); i++) hit = hit || rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom ?? 1, rule.num ?? 1);
      if (hit) { pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1)); state = 'success'; }
      else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'cond': {
      // ItemDropWithConditionRule：条件不过 = DoesntFillConditions（failedRoll 链不进）
      if (!condPass(rule, ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom ?? 1, rule.num ?? 1)) {
        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
```

```
import type { RNG } from '../core/rng';

const NPC_VALUE: Record<string, number> = npcValueJson as any;

/** netID → 规则列表（注册序）；原版 _entriesByNpcNetId */
const rulesByNpc = new Map<number, Rule[]>();
const globalRules: Rule[] = [];
for (const e of ENTRIES) {

    l.push(e.rule);
  }
}

}
```

```
      const it = pick(TRAVEL_POOL.paintings, 0);
      if (canAdd(it)) { stock.push(it); break; }
    }
    return stock.map((id) => [id, ITEM_VALUES[String(id)] ?? 100]);
  }

  /** 黎明自然 roll 命中后的静默开入侵（Main.cs:64933/:64944 直呼 StartInvasion：
   *  invasionType≠0 时内部 no-op，无失败 toast——toast 是物品召唤的 canStartInvasion 语义） */
  private announceNaturalInvasion(type: number): boolean {
    const misc = startInvasion(this.world, type);
    if (misc == null || misc < 0) return false;
    this.invasionWarn = 3600;
    this.newText(Lang.misc(misc), 175, 75, 255);
    return true;
  }

  /** 开启入侵并广播 approaching 公告（StartInvasion + InvasionWarning）。
   *  探针/物品触发共用入口；已进行中返回 false */
  startInvasionAndAnnounce(type: number, ignoreDelay = false): boolean {
    const w = this.world;
    if (!canStartInvasion(w, this.player.maxHp, ignoreDelay)) {
      if (this.player.maxHp < 200) this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.Need200Hp'));
      else this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.BossActive'));
      return false;
    }
    const misc = startInvasion(w, type);
    if (misc == null || misc < 0) return false;
    this.invasionWarn = 3600;
    this.newText(Lang.misc(misc), 175, 75, 255);
    return true;
  }

  /** 季节旗标（checkXMas Main.cs:13054-13073 / checkHalloween :13093-13110：
   *  现实日期 ∪ 月事件胜利当日位 ∪ 永久季种子位 三路或——Forever 随存档 flags 持久化） */
  private checkSeasonal() {
    const now = new Date();
    // 现实日期（checkXMas Main.cs:13054 / checkHalloween :13093）∪ 月事件胜利强制位
    // ∪ forceXMas/HalloweenForever（Main.cs:13071/13104，WorldFile.cs:1418/2459 存档位）
    this.world.clock.xMas = (now.getMonth() === 11 && now.getDate() >= 15)
      || this.forceXMasForToday || !!this.world.flags.forceXMasForever;
    this.world.clock.halloween = (now.getMonth() === 9 && now.getDate() >= 10)
      || (now.getMonth() === 10 && now.getDate() <= 1)
      || this.forceHalloweenForToday || !!this.world.flags.forceHalloweenForever;
  }

```

```
  private fishingCheck(p: Player, game: GameHooks, idx: number): void {

    if (atmo < 0.25) atmo = 0.25;
    if (atmo > 1) atmo = 1;
    const waterNeeded = Math.floor(300 * atmo);       // :19256 waterNeededToFish = 300 × atmo
    if (pond.waterTilesCount / waterNeeded < 1) {

    }

    if (luck < 0) {
      if (Math.random() < -luck) level = Math.floor(level * (0.9 - Math.random() * 0.3));
```

```
}

// ============ 心/星（NPCLoot_DropCommonLifeAndMana :80332 1:1） ============
export function rollHeartsAndStars(ctx: NpcDropCtx): ResolvedDrop[] {
  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
  if (ctx.type !== 16 && ctx.type !== 81 && ctx.type !== 121
    && ctx.lifeMax > 1 && ctx.damage > 0) {
    if (ctx.rng.int(0, 5) === 0) { // RollLuck(6)==0
      if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.mana < ctx.manaMax) drops.push({ id: 184, n: 1 });
      else if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.hp < ctx.hpMax) drops.push({ id: 58, n: 1 });
    }
    if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.mana < ctx.manaMax) drops.push({ id: 184, n: 1 }); // RollLuck(2)==0
  }
  return drops;
}

// ============ Boss 药水+心（DoDeathEvents_DropBossPotionsAndHearts :79790） ============
export function rollBossPotionsAndHearts(ctx: NpcDropCtx): ResolvedDrop[] {
  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
  const stack = ctx.rng.int(5, 15); // Next(5,16)
  let potion = 28; // 弱效治疗药水
  if (ctx.type === 113 || ctx.type === 35 || ctx.type === 668) potion = 188; // 治疗药水
  else if (ctx.type === 222) potion = 1134;
  else if (ctx.type === 657) potion = 499;
  else if (ctx.type > 113 && ctx.type < 222) potion = 499; // 强效治疗
  else if (ctx.type === 636 || ctx.type === 245 || ctx.type === 262 || ctx.type === 370
    || ctx.type === 395 || ctx.type === 439) potion = 499;
  else if (ctx.type === 398) potion = 3544;
  drops.push({ id: potion, n: stack });
  const hearts = ctx.rng.int(5, 9); // Next(5)+5 → 5..9? 原版 Next(5)+5 = 5..9
  for (let i = 0; i < hearts; i++) drops.push({ id: 58, n: 1 });
  return drops;
}

// ============ 掉落初速度（Item.cs:49325-49337） ============
export function dropVelocity(rng: RNG): { vx: number; vy: number } {
  return { vx: rng.int(-30, 30) * 0.1, vy: rng.int(-40, -15) * 0.1 };
}

export { SLIME_BODY };
```

```
        if (this.nebulaCb) this.nebulaCb(); // 星云 booster（:12892-12905，伤害>0 门已过）
        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
        e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game);
        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD060');
        this.hitSet.add(e.id);
        // ---- 吸血链（Projectile.cs:12877-12892 on-hit 段）----
        // 吸血鬼飞刀 304 → vampireHeal（:12879-12882，!moonLeech 门）；
        // 魔法弹 + 幽灵套 ghostHeal → ghostHeal（:12883-12888，canGhostHeal 全 true
        // 近似——雕像产怪 flag 未实装）。ghostHurt（幽灵伤害头 156）未接，登记。
        // numHits = 含本次的命中数（原版 StrikeNPC 先自增，hitSet.size 此时已含本敌）
        if (this.projId === 304) {
          game.applyLifeSteal?.('vampire', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y);
        } else if (projectileData(this.projId)?.magic) {
          game.applyLifeSteal?.('ghost', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y, true);
        }
        if (--this.pierce <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
      }
    }
    // 城镇 NPC 受击(原版 Projectile.Damage 对 friendly NPC 同样生效——杀裁缝师
    // 召骷髅王即此链);穿透/销毁语义与敌怪一致
    if (hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2)
      && --this.pierce <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
  }

  /** aiStyle 14 撞块反弹（Projectile.cs:18314-18327 默认档：法向 ×-0.5，低速归零）。
   *  逐轴回退探测穿透轴并反弹；两轴均无法脱困（嵌入过深）则返回 false 交还消亡。 */
```

```
//         初速 = (Next(-100,101), Next(200)+100) 归一化到恒速 12px/tick（无重力）。
//   Spawner 期 180 tick（ai[0]+=dayRate；定向时 X 向玩家 ×0.35）后转正星。
//   正星（aiStyle 5）：撞实心即 Kill；白天+自然星（damage>500）Kill 不掉落；
//         每 20+Next(40) tick 播 SoundID.Item9。
//   落地（Kill :75593）：自然星掉落 Item 75 坠落之星 ×1；尘埃 58×7 + 圆环 278。
import { Entity } from './Entity';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';

export class FallingStar extends Entity {
  w = 18; h = 18;
  dead = false;
  /** 自然星标记（原版 damage>500；武器星不掉落品/白天不清除） */
  natural: boolean;
  /** 运气定向玩家（原版 ai[1]>-1：X 向玩家衰减追踪） */
  private homing = false;
  /** Spawner 阶段剩余 tick（原版 ai[0] 180 后转正星） */
  private warmup: number;
  private soundT = 0;
  private nextSound: number;

  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, warmup: number, homing: boolean, natural: boolean) {
    super();
    this.x = x; this.y = y;
    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.warmup = warmup;
    this.homing = homing;
    this.natural = natural;
    this.soundT = 0;
    this.nextSound = 20 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 40);
  }

  /** 出生点校验（原版 Collision.SolidCollision 门 :71887） */
  static canSpawnAt(st: GameHooks['world']['store'], x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const tx = Math.floor(x / TILE), ty = Math.floor(y / TILE);
    return st.inBounds(tx, ty) && !st.isSolid(tx, ty);
  }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
    const w = game.world;
    // 白天清除（Spawner :50193 / 正星 :22141——仅自然星）
    if (w.clock.isDay) {
      if (this.natural || this.warmup > 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
    }
    if (this.warmup > 0) {
      // AI_148 Spawner 期：ai[0]+=dayRate；180 后定向时 velocity.X *= 0.35 再按玩家方向
      this.warmup--;
      if (this.warmup === 0) {
        if (this.homing) {
          const p = game.player;
          this.vx = Math.sign(p.cx - this.cx) * Math.abs(this.vx) * 0.35;
          if (this.vx === 0) this.vx = 0.1;
        }
      }
      return; // 悬停期不移动（原版 Spawner tileCollide=false 定点）
    }
=== spawn site ===
```

```
   *  窒息延迟→荆棘族触碰毁块。伤害被完全抵消(含无敌帧)时跳过 buff 授予(原版 num83==0 分支) */
  private suffocateDelay = 0;
  private applyTouchDamage(game: GameHooks, ht: HurtTile): void {
    let blocked = false;
    const dmg = TOUCH_IMMEDIATE.get(ht.type);
    if (dmg !== undefined) {
      this.lastDamageCause = { kind: 'default' }; // PlayerDeathReason.ByOther(3) → 默认包
      if (!this.damage(dmg, this.cx, this.y - 10, false)) {
        blocked = true;
      } else {
        game.playSfx('hurt');
        game.addDamageNumber(this.cx, this.y, Math.max(1, Math.round(dmg - this.defense * 0.5)), false, '#FF5050');
      }
    }
    if (!blocked) {
      if (TOUCH_HOT.has(ht.type)) this.buffs.apply(BuffType.Burning, 20 / 60);   // AddBuff(67,20)
      if (TOUCH_BLEEDING.has(ht.type)) this.buffs.apply(BuffType.Bleeding, (600 + Math.random() * 600) / 60); // AddBuff(30,600-1200)
    }
    if (SUFFOCATE.has(ht.type)) {
      if (this.suffocateDelay < 5) this.suffocateDelay++;       // suffocateDelay<5 递增
      else this.buffs.apply(BuffType.Suffocation, 1 / 60);      // 满后每 tick AddBuff(68,1)
    } else {
      this.suffocateDelay = 0;
    }
    if (TOUCH_DESTROY.has(ht.type)) {
      game.world.breakTileAt(ht.x, ht.y); // TouchDamageDestroyTile → KillTile
    }
  }
```

```
13:**Luck** is a hidden statistic that influences many of _Terraria's_ randomly-chosen items and events, such as the chances of item drops, rare [enemy](Enemy "Enemy") and [critter](Critter "Critter") spawns, certain [town NPCs](Town_NPC.md "Town NPC")' first appearances, or the [Traveling Merchant](Traveling_Merchant.md "Traveling Merchant")'s inventory items. Players can estimate their luck value by speaking to the [Wizard](Wizard.md "Wizard"). 
30:[](Lucky_Coin.md "Lucky Coin")[Lucky Coin](Lucky_Coin.md "Lucky Coin")(and upgrades) | +0.05   
32:[](Coins.md "Coins")[Coins](Coins.md "Coins") | 0 to +0.2 (details)   
46:  * Killing a Ladybug (either naturally spawned or player-released) at a close distance or consuming one as [bait](Bait.md "Bait") during [fishing](Fishing.md "Fishing") causes [](Ladybug.md "Ladybug") −0.2 / [](Gold_Ladybug.md "Gold Ladybug") −0.4 Ladybug luck that increases linearly to 0 over the course of [](Ladybug.md "Ladybug") 3 / [](Gold_Ladybug.md "Gold Ladybug") 6 minutes at most.
52:### Coins
54:Throwing [coins](Coins.md "Coins") into [Shimmer](Shimmer.md "Shimmer") will consume them and increase the "coin luck counter" of all players within a radius of 1000 pixels (62.5 tiles) by a certain amount[1], depending on the type of coin and stack size: 1 Copper Coin increases the coin luck counter by 1.[2]
56:Coin | Coin luck counter   
58:[](Copper_Coin "Copper Coin")[Copper Coin](Copper_Coin "Copper Coin") (1)  | 1   
59:[](Silver_Coin "Silver Coin")[Silver Coin](Silver_Coin "Silver Coin") (1)  | 100   
60:[](Gold_Coin "Gold Coin")[Gold Coin](Gold_Coin "Gold Coin") (1)  | 10,000   
61:[](Platinum_Coin "Platinum Coin")[Platinum Coin](Platinum_Coin "Platinum Coin") (1)  | 1,000,000   
63:The coin luck counter will decrease by 0.01% each tick[3], and it will be immediately set to 0 if it is less than 0.25[4] (most likely because this function, being an exponential degradation function, would otherwise be incapable of reaching zero). The player's remaining coin luck counter grants them a luck boost as displayed in the table below.[5]
65:Remaining coin luck counter (X) | Luck   
75:The coin luck counter can never exceed 1,000,000,[1] hence consuming 1 Platinum Coin fully charges it. This grants the maximum luck boost of +0.2 for 3 minutes 51.7 seconds, after which the boost diminishes by 0.025, then a further 0.025 every 6 minutes 23.75 seconds. 
77:The coin luck counter will decrease faster if the player is speeding up time by sleeping in a [Bed](Bed "Bed"), by using the [Enchanted Sundial](Enchanted_Sundial.md "Enchanted Sundial") or [Enchanted Moondial](Enchanted_Moondial.md "Enchanted Moondial"), or through [Journey Mode](Journey_Mode.md "Journey Mode")'s time speed slider. It does not decrease at all if time is frozen in Journey Mode.[6]
79:Coin luck is reset to 0 as soon as the player leaves the world, but not when the player dies. The coin luck counter also does not decrease while the player is waiting to respawn.[7]
139:As an example, for a drop that has a chance of 1/50 (2%) upon killing an enemy, if that enemy is killed 1000 times, one would expect 20 of that drop on average. 
141:  * After killing a Ladybug, one would instead get 18 (rounding down) of that drop on average. If it took an hour to farm for this drop, it now would take an additional 7 minutes. Note that the negative Ladybug luck would wear out by then.
142:  * In an [Underground Jungle](Underground_Jungle.md "Underground Jungle") biome with a single [Jungle Torch](Jungle_Torch.md "Jungle Torch"), a [Garden Gnome](Garden_Gnome.md "Garden Gnome"), with 5 minutes of the [Lucky](Lucky_\(buff\) "Lucky \(buff\)") buff remaining, on a [Lantern Night](Lantern_Night.md "Lantern Night"), one would get 28 of that drop on average. If it took an hour to farm for this drop, it now would take approximately 17 fewer minutes.
146:### Item drops
148:With a few exceptions, almost all drop rates of items are affected by luck. The luck of the player closest to the defeated enemy is used for the calculation. 
150:  * This includes the chances of enemies dropping [Hearts](Heart.md "Heart") and [Mana Stars](Mana_Star "Mana Star"), as well as the chances of the [Zombie Elf](Zombie_Elf.md "Zombie Elf"), [Scarecrow](Scarecrow.md "Scarecrow"), [Hellhound](Hellhound.md "Hellhound"), and [Poltergeist](Poltergeist.md "Poltergeist") dropping more than one Heart.
151:  * Any time a [boss](Boss "Boss") (or certain other enemy, notably [Mimics](Mimics.md "Mimics") and [Biome Mimics](Biome_Mimic.md "Biome Mimic")) is guaranteed to drop one item from a list, luck does not apply to the choice. The following other boss drops are also unaffected by luck:[10]
158:  * [](Brain_in_a_Jar.md "Brain in a Jar")[Brain in a Jar](Brain_in_a_Jar.md "Brain in a Jar")
193:  * [](Star_Wrath.md "Star Wrath")[Star Wrath](Star_Wrath.md "Star Wrath")
198:  * [](Rainbow_Crystal_Staff.md "Rainbow Crystal Staff")[Rainbow Crystal Staff](Rainbow_Crystal_Staff.md "Rainbow Crystal Staff")
206:  * [](Shrub_Star.md "Shrub Star")[Shrub Star](Shrub_Star.md "Shrub Star")
244:  * Another exception is the [Feather](Feather.md "Feather") dropping from [Harpies](Harpy.md "Harpy"), which always has a 1/2 (50%) chance unaffected by luck.[11]
245:  * Only direct enemy and town NPC drops are affected by luck. The drop rates of items from [grab bags](Grab_bags.md "Grab bags") like [crates](Crates.md "Crates"), [Oysters](Oyster.md "Oyster") and [Treasure Bags](Treasure_Bag.md "Treasure Bag") are unaffected.
246:  * Negative luck provides a chance to increase the number of [Poo](Poo.md "Poo") blocks dropped when sitting on a [Toilet](Toilet "Toilet").[12]
252:  * Golden [critters](Critters.md "Critters") replacing a regular critter spawn (including [shaken trees](Trees#Shaking "Trees")).
268:  * [](Golden_Slime.md "Golden Slime")[Golden Slime](Golden_Slime.md "Golden Slime")
275:  * [](Rainbow_Slime.md "Rainbow Slime")[Rainbow Slime](Rainbow_Slime.md "Rainbow Slime")
288:  * The chance of [Coin Portals](Coin_Portal.md "Coin Portal") appearing from [Pots](Pot.md "Pot") is affected by the luck of the player closest to the broken Pot.[17]
289:  * The player with the highest luck will influence the chances of items being available for sale in the [Traveling Merchant](Traveling_Merchant.md "Traveling Merchant")'s inventory.[18]
290:  * The number of [Copper Coins](Copper_Coin "Copper Coin") dropped when hitting an enemy or NPC with the [Classy Cane](Classy_Cane.md "Classy Cane") is affected by luck. Positive luck gives a chance of [math]\displaystyle{ \text{luck} \times 100\% }[/math] of increasing the maximum number of dropped coins by a random value between 0 and 24, whereas negative luck gives a chance of [math]\displaystyle{ \left|\text{luck}\right| \times 100\% }[/math] of decreasing the maximum number by a random value between 1 and 13.[19]
291:  * While [fishing](Fishing.md "Fishing"), positive luck gives a chance of [math]\displaystyle{ \text{luck} \times 100\% }[/math] of silently multiplying Fishing Power by a random number between 110% and 140%.[20]
292:    * Inversely, negative luck gives a chance of [math]\displaystyle{ -\text{luck} \times 100\% }[/math] of silently multiplying Fishing Power by a random number between 60% and 90%.
293:    * Actual fishing catches are not influenced by luck beyond Fishing Power.
294:  * [Coins](Coins.md "Coins") dropped by enemies are affected by luck, with luck causing the game to generate two coin drop values, choosing the greater one with positive luck and the lesser one with negative luck. How much effect this has depends on external factors such as the [Midas](Midas.md "Midas") debuff and [Blood Moons](Blood_Moon.md "Blood Moon"), as more spread means the two generated values have more variance.
```

```
  /** 瓦罐掉落（SpawnThingsFromPot 肉前普通世界分支，WorldGen.cs:57100-57760） */
  private potLoot(x: number, y: number, style: number) {
    const w = this.world;
    const underworldY = w.store.h - 200; // UnderworldLayer ≈ maxTilesY-200
    const flag = y < w.rockLevel;         // 原版 flag：j < rockLayer（地表+泥土层）
    // item id → 我们 key：VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID 全量映射（vi_ 表覆盖 6146 件）。
    // 原生 def 覆盖段已退役（曾把火把/金币折到本地键,后者还把 73 金折成铜币——既有 bug）
    const drop = (itemId: number, n = 1): ItemDrop | null => {
      const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[itemId];
      if (!key) return null;
      return this.spawnDrop(x * TILE + 8, y * TILE + 8, key, n, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2.4, -2 - Math.random());
    };
    /** 心 pickup（item 58 是 IsAPickup：触碰回血不进包——此处曾误当蘑菇收集物） */
    const dropHeart = () => { const h = drop(58); if (h) h.pickup = 'heart'; };
    const R = (n: number) => (Math.random() * n) | 0;
    // L57213：1/45 稀有 → 按深度三张药水表
    if (R(45) === 0) {
      if (y < w.groundLevel) {
        const n = R(10);
        if (n <= 3) drop([292, 298, 299, 290][n]);
        else if (n === 4) drop(2322);
        else if (n === 5) drop(2324);
        else if (n === 6) drop(2325);
        else drop(2350, 1 + R(2));
      } else if (flag) {
        const n = R(11);
        if (n === 0) drop(289);
        else if (n <= 6) drop([298, 299, 290, 303, 291, 304][n - 1]);
        else if (n === 7) drop(2322);
        else if (n === 8) drop(2329);
        else drop(2350, 1 + R(2));
        if (R(15) === 0) drop(4870);
      } else {
        const n = R(15);
        if (n === 0) drop(296);
        else if (n === 1) drop(295);
        else if (n <= 13) drop([299, 302, 303, 305, 301, 302, 297, 304, 2322, 2323, 2327, 2329][n - 2]);
        else drop(2350, 1 + R(2));
        if (R(15) === 0) drop(4870);
      }
      return;
    }
    // L57505：num10 = rand(7)（expert 才 -1，WorldGen.cs:57482-57486——曾漏实现此偏移）
    const num10 = R(7) - (this.world.isExpert ? 1 : 0);
    // 载入期液体收敛(waterCheck→killTile→breakTile→smashPot)可能砸水边瓦罐,
    // 此时 player 尚未创建(afterWorldLoad 尾部)——无主角视为"受伤"走蘑菇分支,
    // 掉落物随后被 entities.clear() 清掉,无泄漏
    const pl = this.player;
    if (num10 === 0 && (!pl || pl.hp < pl.maxHp)) {
      // 心 pickup（WorldGen.cs:57511-57531）：1 颗 + 1/2 第二颗；
      // 专家模式（大师也算）再 1/2、1/2 两颗
      dropHeart();
      if (R(2) === 0) dropHeart();
      if ((pl && this.world.isExpert)) {   // WorldGen.cs:57520 Main.expertMode（世界难度）
        if (R(2) === 0) dropHeart();
        if (R(2) === 0) dropHeart();
      }
      return;
    }
    if (num10 === 1 || num10 === 0) {
      // 火把（原版 num10==0 时需背包火把<20，火把计数略；液体中→荧光棒 282）
      const inLiquid = w.store.liquid[w.store.idx(x, y)] > 0;
      drop(inLiquid ? 282 : 8, 2 + R(5));
      return;
    }
    switch (num10) {
      case 2: { // 箭 40×10-20；泥土层半数 42；地狱 265（L57617）
        let t = 40;
        if (flag && R(2) === 0) t = 42;
        if (y > underworldY) t = 265;
        drop(t, 10 + R(11));
        return;
      }
      case 3: // 弱效治疗药水 28（地狱/困难→治疗药水 188）（L57625-57638）
        drop(y > underworldY ? 188 : 28, 1);
        return;
      case 4:
      case 5: // 绳 965×20-40（仅非地狱肉前，L57683）
        if (y < underworldY) { drop(965, 20 + R(21)); return; }
        break;
    }
    // 默认：钱币 num16（L57688+：200±100 × 深度系数 × style 系数 × 多级随机加成）
    let coins = 200 + R(201) - 100;
    if (y < w.groundLevel) coins *= 0.5;
    else if (flag) coins *= 0.75;
    else if (y > w.store.h - 250) coins *= 1.25;
    for (const [den, lo, hi] of [[4, 5, 11], [8, 10, 21], [12, 20, 41], [16, 40, 81], [20, 50, 101]] as const) {
      if (R(den) === 0) coins *= 1 + (lo + R(hi - lo)) / 100;
    }
    // style 系数 num（L57113-57160）→ coins *= (num*2+1)/3
    let num = 1;
    if (style >= 4 && style <= 6) num = 1.25;
    else if (style >= 7 && style <= 9) num = 1.75;
    else if (style >= 10 && style <= 12) num = 1.9;
    else if (style >= 13 && style <= 15) num = 2.1;
    else if (style >= 16 && style <= 18) num = 1.6;
    else if (style >= 19 && style <= 21) num = 3.5;
    else if (style >= 22 && style <= 24) num = 1.6;
    else if (style >= 25 && style <= 27) num = 10;
    else if (style >= 28 && style <= 30) num = 4; // hardMode 才 ×4，肉前应为 1——此处取肉前
    else if (style >= 31 && style <= 33) num = 2;
    else if (style >= 34 && style <= 36) num = 1.25;
    coins *= (num * 2 + 1) / 3;
    if (coins > 1000) coins = 1000; // 防御性上限（原版由 Item.NewItem 内部面值转换）
    drop(73, Math.max(1, Math.round(coins)));
  }
```

```
      if (!rule.ids?.length) {
        if (rule._unresolved) warnOnce(`unresolved:${rule._unresolved}`, `oneOf 池未解析：${rule._unresolved}（跳过）`);
        state = 'failedRoll';
        break;
      }
      if (tryRoll(ctx, rule)) {
        pushDrop(out, rule.ids[ctx.rng.int(0, rule.ids.length - 1)], 1);
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedRoll';
      break;
    }
    case 'expert': { // DropBasedOnExpertMode：normal/expert 二选一委托
      state = evalRule(ctx.isExpert ? rule.expert : rule.normal, ctx, out);
      break;
    }
    case 'master': { // DropBasedOnMasterMode
      state = evalRule(ctx.isMaster ? rule.master : rule.def, ctx, out);
      break;
    }
    case 'masterExpert': { // DropBasedOnMasterAndExpertMode：master>expert>def

    }
    case 'bossBag': {
      // BossBag = DropBasedOnExpertMode(DropNothing, local(bag)+value=0)；
      // DropNothing 状态 = DoesntFillConditions（非专家分支不进 OnSuccess 链）
      if (rule.cond && !evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
      if (ctx.isExpert) {
        // local 规则 rng.Next(1)<1 恒过 → 掉袋 + 清 value
        pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1);
        out.zeroValue = true;
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedCond';
      break;
    }
    case 'masterCommon': { // ByCondition(IsMasterMode, item)（非 master = DoesntFillConditions）
      if (ctx.isMaster) { pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1); state = 'success'; }
      else state = 'failedCond';
      break;
    }
    case 'masterAll': { // DropBasedOnMasterMode(DropNothing, perPlayer(item, denom, IsMasterMode))
      if (ctx.isMaster) {
        // DropPerPlayerOnThePlayer → CommonCode.DropItemForEachInteractingPlayerOnThePlayer
        // （CommonCode.cs:57-70）：掷骰 rng.Next(denom)<1（不走 Luck）；且无论掷中与否
        // 收尾 `npc.value = 0f`——即大师模式 Boss 掉宠物这条恒清钱币（曾漏：宠物+钱币双掉）
        out.zeroValue = true;
        if ((rule.denom ?? 1) > 1 && ctx.rng.int(0, (rule.denom ?? 1) - 1) < 1) {

      } else state = 'failedCond';

    }
    case 'local': case 'perPlayer': {

      if (rule.cond && !evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
      if (ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1, rule.denom ?? 1) - 1) < 1) {

        out.zeroValue = true;
        state = 'success';
      } else state = 'failedRoll';

    }

    case 'noRepeat': { // FromOptionsWithoutRepeatsDropRule.cs:27-45：
      // CanDrop 恒 true；dropCount 次不放回抽样（每件 rng.Next(len) 取一后移除），
      // 每件数量恒 1；规则状态恒 Success（无掷骰）
      {
        const pool = [...(rule.ids as number[])];
        for (let i = 0; i < (rule.count ?? 0); i++) {
          if (pool.length <= 0) break;
          const idx = ctx.rng.int(0, pool.length - 1);
          pushDrop(out, pool[idx], 1);
          pool.splice(idx, 1);
        }
        state = 'success';
      }
      break;
    }
    default:
      // 未知 kind：按 DoesntFillConditions 处理（曾返回 success——会让挂在它身上的
      // OnSuccess 链整支误触发；failedCond 则三条链全部不进，语义最保守）
      warnOnce(`rule:${rule.k}`, `未实现的规则 kind ${rule.k}（按 failedCond 跳过）`);
      state = 'failedCond';
      break;
  }
  // 链：按父状态门控（Chains.cs TryIfSucceeded/TryIfFailedRandomRoll/TryIfDoesntFillConditions）
  for (const c of rule.chain ?? []) {
    if ((c.on === 'success' && state === 'success')
      || (c.on === 'failedRoll' && state === 'failedRoll')
      || (c.on === 'failedConditions' && state === 'failedCond')) {
      evalRule(c.rule, ctx, out);
    }
  }
  return state;
}
```

```
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.ItemDropRules;

public class ItemDropWithConditionRule : CommonDrop
{
	public IItemDropRuleCondition condition;

	public ItemDropWithConditionRule(int itemId, int chanceDenominator, int amountDroppedMinimum, int amountDroppedMaximum, IItemDropRuleCondition condition, int chanceNumerator = 1)
		: base(itemId, chanceDenominator, amountDroppedMinimum, amountDroppedMaximum, chanceNumerator)
	{
		this.condition = condition;
	}

	public override bool CanDrop(DropAttemptInfo info)
	{
		return condition.CanDrop(info);
	}

	public override void ReportDroprates(List<DropRateInfo> drops, DropRateInfoChainFeed ratesInfo)
	{
		DropRateInfoChainFeed ratesInfo2 = ratesInfo.With(1f);
		ratesInfo2.AddCondition(condition);
		float num = (float)chanceNumerator / (float)chanceDenominator;
		float dropRate = num * ratesInfo2.parentDroprateChance;
		drops.Add(new DropRateInfo(itemId, amountDroppedMinimum, amountDroppedMaximum, dropRate, ratesInfo2.conditions));
		Chains.ReportDroprates(base.ChainedRules, num, drops, ratesInfo2);
	}
}

```

```
Now I have everything. Let me do a final sanity check on two items:
1. The repo `rollLuck` in NpcDrops.ts: mathematically — vanilla `Luck.RollLuck(luck, range)`:
   - luck>0 && NextFloat()<luck → `Next(Next(range/2, range))` — i.e., inner roll over [range/2, range), then outer roll [0, inner). Repo: `inner = rng.int(floor(denom/2), denom-1); return rng.int(0, max(1,inner)-1) < num`. Equivalent (assuming rng.int is inclusive). ✅
   - luck<0 → `Next(Next(range, range*2))`: inner in [range, 2*range), outer [0, inner). Repo: `inner = rng.int(denom, denom*2-1); rng.int(0, max(1,inner)-1) < num`. ✅

2. NpcDrops.ts rollLuck condition `if (luck !== 0 && ctx.rng.next() < Math.abs(luck))` — vanilla: `if (luck > 0f && Main.rand.NextFloat() < luck)` / `if (luck < 0f && ...)`. Same net. ✅
```

```
## 三、最需修复 TOP3

1. **伤害链 DamageVar 的 luck 参数全缺(#2/#3)** — 影响面最大:玩家每一次受击(接触/敌弹/地刺)与每一次输出(近战/弹幕)。且 NPC→玩家接触伤目前连 ±15% 基础浮动都没有(`Enemy.ts:765` 传 `def.damage` 原值),这是超出 luck 之外的独立原版行为缺口。建议在 `core` 加 `damageVar(dmg, luck)`(对应 `Main.cs:65597`),接入 `damagePlayer`、`hitPlayer`、`Arrow` 命中与 `Game.ts:7605` 四点。
2. **刷怪侧 RollLuck 整族未接(#6/#7)** — 原版 NPC.cs 66 处 RollLuck 全靠 `NPC.luck=player.luck` 驱动,金小动物(1/400)、绑缚史莱姆、地精、稀有怪全部不受幸运影响。修法机械:给 `VanillaSpawner.spawn` 加 luck 形参,`N(n)` 换成 `rollLuck(luck,n)===0`,并顺手补上已登记的 Groom/Bride `rollOnlyBadLuck(300)`(`Luck.ts` 里现成函数,spawner 侧零引用)。
3. **三处低成本精确修(#9/#1c/#12)** — `rollHeartsAndStars` 改用 ctx.luck 的 rollLuck(一行);`extract-npcdrops.mjs:286-288` 把 ScalingWithOnlyBadLuck 单列 `onlyBad:true` 并在 `NpcDrops.ts` 加 `rollOnlyBadLuck` 分支(否则正运玩家货郎背包掉率被虚增);`Weather.startRain` 金币雨门换成 `rollLuck(luck,25)===0`。
```
